1. Install

Run this complete block in PowerShell:

& {
    $PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
    $PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1"

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    try {
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT)) {
            throw "Project root does not exist: $PROJECT_ROOT"
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Copy-Item `
                -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
                -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
                -Force

            if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
                throw "The patch ZIP could not be staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
            }

            Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
            throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP"
        }

        Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

        $Archive = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($WORK_PATCH_ZIP)
        $Seen = New-Object `
            'System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]' `
            ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

        try {
            foreach ($Entry in $Archive.Entries) {
                $Name = [string]$Entry.FullName

                if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Name)) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
                }

                if ($Name.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character in member name"
                }

                $Normalized = $Name.Replace('\', '/')

                if (
                    $Normalized.StartsWith('/') -or
                    $Normalized.StartsWith('//') -or
                    $Normalized -match '^[A-Za-z]:'
                ) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $Name"
                }

                if ($Normalized.Split('/') -contains '..') {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $Name"
                }

                if (-not $Seen.Add($Normalized)) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $Name"
                }
            }
        }
        finally {
            $Archive.Dispose()
        }

        Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
            Remove-Item `
                -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
                -Recurse `
                -Force
        }

        New-Item `
            -ItemType Directory `
            -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Force | Out-Null

        Expand-Archive `
            -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Force

        $Installer = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Installer)) {
            throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found in the extracted staged patch."
        }

        & $Installer `
            -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -NoTerminalCleanup

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Installer returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
        }

        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "INSTALL OK: brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-prompt-registration-v1"
        Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
        Clear-Host
    }
    catch {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
        Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
        Write-Host ""
        Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
        Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
        Clear-Host
    }
}

2. Validate and prepare freeze

Run this only after installation succeeds:

& {
    $PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
    $PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1"
    $FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-prompt-registration-v1"

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE_PREP = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    try {
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
            throw "Staged patch ZIP was not found: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        }

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $VALIDATOR)) {
            throw "VALIDATE.ps1 was not found: $VALIDATOR"
        }

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE_PREP)) {
            throw "FREEZE.ps1 was not found: $FREEZE_PREP"
        }

        & $VALIDATOR `
            -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -NoTerminalCleanup

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Validation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
        }

        & $FREEZE_PREP `
            -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -NoTerminalCleanup

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Freeze preparation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
        }

        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "VALIDATE + FREEZE PREPARATION: PASS"
        Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
        Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "Next human actions:"
        Write-Host "1. Review the pending Error Memory lesson."
        Write-Host "2. Memorize it only after confirming the evidence."
        Write-Host "3. Open Freeze Feature After Update."
        Write-Host "4. Use Preview."
        Write-Host "5. Review the exact current evidence."
        Write-Host "6. Use Confirm and Write manually."
    }
    catch {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "VALIDATION / FREEZE PREPARATION ERROR"
        Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
        Write-Host "Freeze remains blocked."
        Write-Host "Do not use Confirm and Write."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

INSTALL FAILED
Cannot bind argument to parameter 'LiteralPath' because it is null.

The v1 installer has a real delivery defect. A derived path reached -LiteralPath as null, and the old diagnostic did not identify which variable or phase caused it.

I replaced it with a cumulative v1r1 package that:

    resolves the drive from the actual PowerShell project item;

    checks every critical path before any -LiteralPath call;

    reports the failing phase, exception type, message, and command position;

    accepts both an untouched project and a partially installed v1 state;

    stages a new evidence-backed Error Memory lesson;

    preserves manual Freeze Preview and Confirm and Write.

Do not run the old v1 ZIP again.
Corrected files

SHA-256:

927a8f73a89708d130f1629205366212f43da1c4b5de713cf04a78f08edbcc01

Place the new ZIP here:

E:\kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r1.zip

Do not extract it manually.
Install

& {
    $PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
    $PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r1"
    $PHASE = "initialize"

    try {
        $PHASE = "resolve_project_root"

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_ROOT)) {
            throw "PROJECT_ROOT is null or empty."
        }

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -PathType Container)) {
            throw "Project root does not exist: $PROJECT_ROOT"
        }

        $PROJECT_ROOT = (
            Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
        ).Path

        $PROJECT_ITEM = Get-Item `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        $DRIVE_ROOT = [string]$PROJECT_ITEM.PSDrive.Root

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($DRIVE_ROOT)) {
            throw "DRIVE_ROOT is null or empty."
        }

        $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_NAME)) {
            throw "PROJECT_NAME is null or empty."
        }

        $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
            $DRIVE_ROOT `
            ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

        $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
            $DRIVE_ROOT `
            ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

        $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
            $WORK_DIR `
            ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

        $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

        $PHASE = "stage_patch_zip"

        New-Item `
            -ItemType Directory `
            -Path $WORK_DIR `
            -Force | Out-Null

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
            Copy-Item `
                -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
                -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
                -Force

            if (-not (
                Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
            )) {
                throw "The patch ZIP could not be staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
            }

            Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw (
                "Patch ZIP was not found at root or staged location. " +
                "Root: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP Staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
            )
        }

        $PHASE = "zip_member_prevalidation"

        Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

        $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
            $WORK_PATCH_ZIP
        )

        $SEEN = New-Object `
            'System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]' `
            ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

        try {
            foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
                $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

                if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
                }

                if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character in member name"
                }

                $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace('\', '/')

                if (
                    $NORMALIZED.StartsWith('/') -or
                    $NORMALIZED.StartsWith('//') -or
                    $NORMALIZED -match '^[A-Za-z]:'
                ) {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
                }

                if ($NORMALIZED.Split('/') -contains '..') {
                    throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
                }

                if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                    throw (
                        "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        finally {
            $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
        }

        Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

        $PHASE = "extract_staged_patch"

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
            Remove-Item `
                -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
                -Recurse `
                -Force
        }

        New-Item `
            -ItemType Directory `
            -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Force | Out-Null

        Expand-Archive `
            -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Force

        $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
        }

        $PHASE = "run_installer"

        & $INSTALLER `
            -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -NoTerminalCleanup

        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host (
            "INSTALL OK: " +
            "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-" +
            "prompt-registration-v1r1"
        )
        Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
        Clear-Host
    }
    catch {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
        Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
        Write-Host (
            "ERROR TYPE: " +
            $_.Exception.GetType().FullName
        )
        Write-Host (
            "ERROR MESSAGE: " +
            $_.Exception.Message
        )

        if (
            $_.InvocationInfo -and
            $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
        ) {
            Write-Host $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
        }

        Write-Host ""
        Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
        Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
        Clear-Host
    }
}

At line:1 char:1

Unexpected token '}' in expression or statement.
    + CategoryInfo          : ParserError: (:) [], ParentContainsErrorRecordException

The error occurred before installation started. PowerShell received:

}& {

instead of:

& {

There is one extra closing brace } before the command, usually because the second block was pasted while PowerShell was still showing the continuation prompt >>.
Fix

    Press Ctrl+C.

    Close that PowerShell window.

    Open a new PowerShell window.

    Confirm the prompt looks like:

PS C:\...>

It must not show:

>>

    Paste the installation block again, beginning exactly with:

& {

There must be no }, >>, or other character before &.

Run the blocks in order. Use a fresh PowerShell window showing a normal PS ...> prompt, not >>. The sequence follows the uploaded delivery model: install first, validate next, prepare freeze only after validation, and keep final freeze and Error Memory memorization under human control.
1. Install

Copy this entire block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r2"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-prompt-registration-v1r2"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    $PHASE = "verify_root_zip"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "stage_patch_zip"

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Copy-Item `
        -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
        -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -Force

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    Remove-Item `
        -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
        -Force

    Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
    Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $WORK_PATCH_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract_patch"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "run_installer"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

    if (
        $_.InvocationInfo -and
        $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    ) {
        Write-Host $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

try {

    ).Path

    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `

    Write-Host "8. Use Confirm and Write manually."
}
catch {

    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

1. Install v1r3

Paste this entire block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r3"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-prompt-registration-v1r3"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_project_root"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -PathType Container
    )) {
        throw "Project root does not exist: $PROJECT_ROOT"
    }

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_NAME)) {
        throw "PROJECT_NAME is null or empty."
    }

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($DRIVE_ROOT)) {
        throw "DRIVE_ROOT is null or empty."
    }

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    $PHASE = "stage_patch_zip"

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "The patch ZIP could not be staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw (
            "Patch ZIP was not found at the drive root or staged location. " +
            "Root: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP Staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        )
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_prevalidation"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $WORK_PATCH_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract_staged_patch"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "run_installer"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Installer returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

    if (
        $_.InvocationInfo -and
        $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    ) {
        Write-Host $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

        throw "PROJECT_ROOT is null or empty."
    }

        throw "Project root does not exist: $PROJECT_ROOT"
    }

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_NAME)) {
        throw "PROJECT_NAME is null or empty."
    }

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($DRIVE_ROOT)) {
        throw "DRIVE_ROOT is null or empty."
    }

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `

        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    $PHASE = "stage_patch_zip"

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `

        -Force | Out-Null

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "The patch ZIP could not be staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw (
            "Patch ZIP was not found at the drive root or staged location. " +
            "Root: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP Staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        )
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_prevalidation"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $WORK_PATCH_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract_staged_patch"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "run_installer"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host (
        "INSTALL OK: " +
        "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-" +
        "prompt-registration-v1r4"
    )
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

    if (
        $_.InvocationInfo -and
        $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    ) {
        Write-Host $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r5"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_project_root"

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_ROOT)) {
        throw "PROJECT_ROOT is null or empty."
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Project root does not exist: $PROJECT_ROOT"
    }

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($PROJECT_NAME)) {
        throw "PROJECT_NAME is null or empty."
    }

    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($DRIVE_ROOT)) {
        throw "DRIVE_ROOT is null or empty."
    }

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    $PHASE = "stage_patch_zip"

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "The patch ZIP could not be staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw (
            "Patch ZIP was not found at the drive root or staged location. " +
            "Root: $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP Staged: $WORK_PATCH_ZIP"
        )
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_prevalidation"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $WORK_PATCH_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character in member name"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract_staged_patch"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "run_installer"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $WORK_PATCH_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host (
        "INSTALL OK: " +
        "brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-" +
        "prompt-registration-v1r5"
    )
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

    if (
        $_.InvocationInfo -and
        $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    ) {
        Write-Host $_.InvocationInfo.PositionMessage
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE_PREP -PathType Leaf

    & $FREEZE_PREP `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `

}

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import sys

project_root = Path(sys.argv[1]).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
project_root_text = str(project_root)

if project_root_text not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root_text)

from scripts.merge_freeze_validation_evidence import main

arguments = [
    "--project-root",
    project_root_text,
    "--feature-id",
    sys.argv[2],
    "--feature-title",
    sys.argv[3],
    "--patch-zip",
    sys.argv[4],
    "--evidence-file",
    sys.argv[5],
]

raise SystemExit(main(arguments))
'@

    $Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(
        $MERGE_HELPER,
        $MergeHelperText,
        $Utf8NoBom
    )

    & python `
        $MERGE_HELPER `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        $FEATURE_ID `
        $FEATURE_TITLE `
        $PATCH_ZIP `
        $EVIDENCE_FILE

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Canonical freeze-evidence merge returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    $LatestFeatureId = [string]$LatestHintObject.hint.feature_id
    $LatestPatchZip = [string]$LatestHintObject.hint.source_patch_zip
    $LatestEvidence = [string](
        $LatestHintObject.form_inputs.validation_evidence_summary
    )

    if ($LatestFeatureId -ne $FEATURE_ID) {
        throw (

        )

}

Open a fresh PowerShell window and paste this complete block:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$EXPECTED_FREEZE_ID = (
    "freeze-20260714-brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-" +
    "prompt-registration-v1r5-exact-validator-hash-recovery-v2"
)
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $SUPPORT_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_show_project_to_AI")

    $FIRST_PROMPT_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "first_prompt_files"

    $STARTUP_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $FIRST_PROMPT_ROOT `
        "first_prompts_to_ai.zip"

    $GENERATOR = Join-Path `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        "kanda_prompt_workspace\prompt_tools\sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py"

    $FREEZE_CONTEXT_SOURCE = Join-Path `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        "kanda_prompt_workspace\prompt_tools\startup_freeze_context.py"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $GENERATOR -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Startup generator not found: $GENERATOR"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE_CONTEXT_SOURCE -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Startup freeze-context source not found: $FREEZE_CONTEXT_SOURCE"
    }

    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT
    $env:PYTHONPATH = $PROJECT_ROOT

    $PHASE = "python_compile"

    python -m py_compile `
        $GENERATOR `
        $FREEZE_CONTEXT_SOURCE

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup generator py_compile failed."
    }

    Write-Host "STARTUP GENERATOR COMPILE: PASS"

    $PHASE = "ensure_sync"

    python $GENERATOR `
        --ensure-sync `
        --yes `
        --project-root $PROJECT_ROOT

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup ensure-sync failed with code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    $PHASE = "final_sync_check"

    python $GENERATOR `
        --check `
        --project-root $PROJECT_ROOT

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup check failed after ensure-sync."
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STARTUP_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Generated startup ZIP not found: $STARTUP_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "inspect_startup_zip"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $Archive = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STARTUP_ZIP
    )

    try {
        $FreezeEntry = $Archive.Entries |
            Where-Object {
                $_.FullName.Replace("\", "/") -eq
                "09_active_project_freeze_context.md"
            } |
            Select-Object -First 1

        if ($null -eq $FreezeEntry) {
            throw (
                "09_active_project_freeze_context.md " +
                "was not found inside the startup ZIP."
            )
        }

        $Stream = $FreezeEntry.Open()
        $Reader = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader(
            $Stream,
            [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8,
            $true
        )

        try {
            $FreezeContextText = $Reader.ReadToEnd()
        }
        finally {
            $Reader.Dispose()
            $Stream.Dispose()
        }
    }
    finally {
        $Archive.Dispose()
    }

    if (-not $FreezeContextText.Contains($EXPECTED_FREEZE_ID)) {
        throw (
            "Startup freeze context does not contain the newest v2 entry: " +
            $EXPECTED_FREEZE_ID
        )
    }

    $RequiredMarkers = @(
        "ZIP CONTRACT: PASS",
        "VALIDATION OK: brick-wall-comprehensive-quality-gate-prompt-registration-v1r5",
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    foreach ($Marker in $RequiredMarkers) {
        if (-not $FreezeContextText.Contains($Marker)) {
            throw "Startup freeze context marker missing: $Marker"
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "STARTUP FREEZE CONTEXT REFRESH: PASS"
    Write-Host "STARTUP FREEZE CONTEXT V2 ENTRY: PASS"
    Write-Host "LATEST FROZEN ENTRY:"
    Write-Host $EXPECTED_FREEZE_ID
    Write-Host "STARTUP ZIP:"
    Write-Host $STARTUP_ZIP
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "STARTUP FREEZE CONTEXT REFRESH FAILED"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the output, then press Enter"
Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
Clear-Host

Open a fresh PowerShell window and paste this entire block at once. It writes only to the project-support pending-intake folder and uses the exact staged v1r5 ZIP as its source.

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_prompt_registration_v1r5"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $DAILY_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $SUPPORT_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_show_project_to_AI")

    $PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DAILY_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $TEMP_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DAILY_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + "_error_memory_restage")

    $PENDING_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "project_error_memory\pending_ai_assisted_error_lesson_intake"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Staged v1r5 ZIP not found: $PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "extract_error_memory_payload"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $TEMP_ROOT) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $TEMP_ROOT `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $TEMP_ROOT `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $PATCH_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $TEMP_ROOT `
        -Force

    $SOURCE_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $TEMP_ROOT `
        "error_memory_receive_blocks"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $SOURCE_ROOT -PathType Container
    )) {
        throw (
            "Error Memory payload folder was not found in the ZIP: " +
            $SOURCE_ROOT
        )
    }

    $LESSONS = @(
        Get-ChildItem `
            -LiteralPath $SOURCE_ROOT `
            -Filter "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt" `
            -File
    )

    if ($LESSONS.Count -ne 5) {
        throw (
            "Expected exactly 5 Error Memory lessons in v1r5, found: " +
            $LESSONS.Count
        )
    }

    $ExpectedLessonIds = @(
        "lesson-brick-wall-focused-validator-task-route-key-assumption-v1",
        "lesson-brick-wall-install-literalpath-null-guard-and-provenance-v1",
        "lesson-powershell-continuation-prompt-concatenated-scriptblock-v1",
        "lesson-brick-wall-validator-baseline-partial-install-semantic-recovery-v1",
        "lesson-brick-wall-validator-exact-diagnostic-hash-recovery-v1"
    )

    $CombinedText = ""

    foreach ($Lesson in $LESSONS) {
        $Text = Get-Content `
            -LiteralPath $Lesson.FullName `
            -Raw `
            -Encoding UTF8

        $BeginCount = (
            [regex]::Matches(
                $Text,
                "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN"
            )
        ).Count

        $EndCount = (
            [regex]::Matches(
                $Text,
                "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END"
            )
        ).Count

        if ($BeginCount -ne 1 -or $EndCount -ne 1) {
            throw (
                "Invalid Error Memory marker contract: " +
                $Lesson.Name
            )
        }

        $CombinedText += $Text
        $CombinedText += "`n"
    }

    foreach ($LessonId in $ExpectedLessonIds) {
        if (-not $CombinedText.Contains($LessonId)) {
            throw "Expected lesson ID missing from ZIP: $LessonId"
        }
    }

    Write-Host "ERROR MEMORY ZIP PAYLOAD: PASS"
    Write-Host "ERROR MEMORY LESSON COUNT: 5"

    $PHASE = "stage_pending_lessons"

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $PENDING_ROOT `
        -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($Lesson in $LESSONS) {
        $Destination = Join-Path `
            $PENDING_ROOT `
            $Lesson.Name

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Destination -PathType Leaf) {
            $SourceHash = (
                Get-FileHash `
                    -LiteralPath $Lesson.FullName `
                    -Algorithm SHA256
            ).Hash

            $DestinationHash = (
                Get-FileHash `
                    -LiteralPath $Destination `
                    -Algorithm SHA256
            ).Hash

            if ($SourceHash -ne $DestinationHash) {
                throw (
                    "Pending lesson filename collision with different " +
                    "content: $Destination"
                )
            }

            Write-Host ("ALREADY STAGED: " + $Lesson.Name)
        }
        else {
            Copy-Item `
                -LiteralPath $Lesson.FullName `
                -Destination $Destination

            Write-Host ("STAGED: " + $Lesson.Name)
        }
    }

    $PHASE = "verify_pending_folder"

    $PendingLessons = @(
        Get-ChildItem `
            -LiteralPath $PENDING_ROOT `
            -Filter "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt" `
            -File |
            Where-Object {
                $Content = Get-Content `
                    -LiteralPath $_.FullName `
                    -Raw `
                    -Encoding UTF8

                $ExpectedLessonIds |
                    Where-Object { $Content.Contains($_) }
            }
    )

    $MatchedNames = @(
        foreach ($File in Get-ChildItem `
            -LiteralPath $PENDING_ROOT `
            -Filter "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_*.txt" `
            -File
        ) {
            $Content = Get-Content `
                -LiteralPath $File.FullName `
                -Raw `
                -Encoding UTF8

            foreach ($LessonId in $ExpectedLessonIds) {
                if ($Content.Contains($LessonId)) {
                    $File.Name
                    break
                }
            }
        }
    )

    $MatchedNames = @($MatchedNames | Sort-Object -Unique)

    if ($MatchedNames.Count -ne 5) {
        throw (
            "Pending intake verification expected 5 Brick Wall lessons, " +
            "found: " +
            $MatchedNames.Count
        )
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "ERROR MEMORY LESSONS STAGED: PASS"
    Write-Host "BRICK WALL ERROR MEMORY LESSON COUNT: 5"
    Write-Host "PENDING INTAKE FOLDER:"
    Write-Host $PENDING_ROOT
    Write-Host ""

    foreach ($Name in $MatchedNames) {
        Write-Host ("PENDING: " + $Name)
    }

    Remove-Item `
        -LiteralPath $TEMP_ROOT `
        -Recurse `
        -Force

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "ERROR MEMORY RESTAGE COMPLETE: PASS"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "ERROR MEMORY RESTAGE FAILED"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the output, then press Enter"
Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
Clear-Host

1. Place the ZIP

2. Install

Paste this complete block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q01_verified_problem_admission_enforcement_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q01-verified-problem-admission-enforcement-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"
    $PROJECT_ROOT = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT -ErrorAction Stop).Path
    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")
    $ROOT_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    $PHASE = "stage_zip"
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Destination $STAGED_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $STAGED_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_ZIP or $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STAGED_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "install"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host ""

    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Open another fresh PowerShell window and paste this complete block:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q01_verified_problem_admission_enforcement_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q01-verified-problem-admission-enforcement-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Staged ZIP was not found: $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $VALIDATOR -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "VALIDATE.ps1 was not found: $VALIDATOR"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "FREEZE.ps1 was not found: $FREEZE"
    }

    $PHASE = "validation"

    & $VALIDATOR `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

    & $FREEZE `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATE + FREEZE PREPARATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Human actions required:"
    Write-Host "1. Review the pending Error Memory lesson."
    Write-Host "2. Choose Memorize or Reject manually."
    Write-Host "3. Open Freeze Feature After Update."
    Write-Host "4. Use New Local Freeze Entry -> Preview."
    Write-Host "5. Confirm and Write only after current evidence is visible."
    Write-Host "6. Refresh startup freeze context after the write."
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION / FREEZE PREPARATION ERROR"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host "Freeze remains blocked. Do not use Confirm and Write."
}

Write-Host ""

Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
Clear-Host

try {

    $WORK_ROOT = Join-Path `

        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_ROOT = Join-Path $WORK_ROOT $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATE_SCRIPT = Join-Path $EXTRACT_ROOT "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE_SCRIPT = Join-Path $EXTRACT_ROOT "FREEZE.ps1"

    $EVIDENCE_FILE = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "project_validation_evidence\$FEATURE_ID.txt"

    $LATEST_HINT = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "project_freeze_after_update\freeze_hint_intake\latest_freeze_hint.json"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Staged patch ZIP was not found: $PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $VALIDATE_SCRIPT -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "VALIDATE.ps1 was not found: $VALIDATE_SCRIPT"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE_SCRIPT -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "FREEZE.ps1 was not found: $FREEZE_SCRIPT"
    }

    $PHASE = "run_local_validation"

    & $VALIDATE_SCRIPT `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $PATCH_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $EVIDENCE_FILE -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Durable validation evidence was not created: $EVIDENCE_FILE"
    }

    $EVIDENCE_TEXT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $EVIDENCE_FILE `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    $REQUIRED_VALIDATION_MARKERS = @(
        "ZIP CONTRACT: PASS",
        "Q01_VERIFIED_PROBLEM_RECORD: PASS",
        "Q01_RECORD_FIELD_SET: PASS",
        "Q01_EXISTING_CAPABILITY_COMPARISON: PASS",
        "Q01_DUPLICATION_RISK_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_SMALLEST_INTERVENTION_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_MEASURABLE_GAIN_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_DISCONFIRMATION_SEARCH: PASS",
        "Q01_ADMISSION_DECISION_SET: PASS",
        "Q01_ROUTER_BRIDGE_ENFORCEMENT: PASS",
        "Q01_IMPLEMENTATION_GATE_LINK: PASS",
        "Q01_NO_NEW_ENGINE_OR_SCHEMA: PASS",
        "Q01_VERSION_ALIGNMENT: PASS",
        "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID",
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED_VALIDATION_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $EVIDENCE_TEXT.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Validation evidence marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q01 LOCAL VALIDATION EVIDENCE: PASS"

    $PHASE = "canonical_freeze_evidence_merge"

    & $FREEZE_SCRIPT `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `

    }

    $HINT_TEXT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $LATEST_HINT `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    $REQUIRED_HINT_MARKERS = @(
        $PATCH_NAME + ".zip",
        "ZIP CONTRACT: PASS",
        "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID",
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED_HINT_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $HINT_TEXT.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest freeze hint marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    $BLOCKED_MARKERS = @(
        "FREEZE BLOCKED",
        "Pre-validation delivery note only",
        "No recognizable validation evidence",
        "User-local validation is pending"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $BLOCKED_MARKERS) {
        if ($HINT_TEXT.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest freeze hint contains blocked text: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q01 VALIDATION + FREEZE PREPARATION: PASS"

    Write-Host (

    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"

Open a fresh PowerShell window and paste the entire block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q01-verified-problem-admission-enforcement-v1"
$FEATURE_TITLE = "Brick Wall Q01 Verified-Problem Admission Enforcement v1"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q01_verified_problem_admission_enforcement_v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $DAILY_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $SUPPORT_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_show_project_to_AI")

    $PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DAILY_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EVIDENCE_FILE = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "project_validation_evidence\$FEATURE_ID.txt"

    $HINT_ROOT = Join-Path `
        $SUPPORT_ROOT `
        "project_freeze_after_update\freeze_hint_intake"

    $LATEST_HINT = Join-Path `
        $HINT_ROOT `
        "latest_freeze_hint.json"

    $MERGE_MODULE = Join-Path `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        "scripts\merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py"

    $MERGE_HELPER = Join-Path `
        $DAILY_ROOT `
        "q01_merge_freeze_validation_evidence.py"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATCH_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Staged Q01 ZIP not found: $PATCH_ZIP"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $EVIDENCE_FILE -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Q01 validation evidence not found: $EVIDENCE_FILE"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $MERGE_MODULE -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Canonical merge owner not found: $MERGE_MODULE"
    }

    $PHASE = "validation_evidence_preflight"

    $EVIDENCE_TEXT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $EVIDENCE_FILE `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    $REQUIRED_EVIDENCE_MARKERS = @(
        "ZIP CONTRACT: PASS",
        "Q01_VERIFIED_PROBLEM_RECORD: PASS",
        "Q01_RECORD_FIELD_SET: PASS",
        "Q01_EXISTING_CAPABILITY_COMPARISON: PASS",
        "Q01_DUPLICATION_RISK_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_SMALLEST_INTERVENTION_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_MEASURABLE_GAIN_GATE: PASS",
        "Q01_DISCONFIRMATION_SEARCH: PASS",
        "Q01_ADMISSION_DECISION_SET: PASS",
        "Q01_ROUTER_BRIDGE_ENFORCEMENT: PASS",
        "Q01_IMPLEMENTATION_GATE_LINK: PASS",
        "Q01_NO_NEW_ENGINE_OR_SCHEMA: PASS",
        "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID",
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED_EVIDENCE_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $EVIDENCE_TEXT.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Validation evidence marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    Write-Host "Q01 VALIDATION EVIDENCE PREFLIGHT: PASS"

    $PHASE = "create_merge_helper"

    $MERGE_HELPER_TEXT = @'
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import sys

project_root = Path(sys.argv[1]).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
project_root_text = str(project_root)

if project_root_text not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root_text)

from scripts.merge_freeze_validation_evidence import main

arguments = [
    "--project-root",
    project_root_text,
    "--feature-id",
    sys.argv[2],
    "--feature-title",
    sys.argv[3],
    "--patch-zip",
    sys.argv[4],
    "--evidence-file",
    sys.argv[5],
]

raise SystemExit(main(arguments))
'@

    $UTF8_NO_BOM = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(
        $MERGE_HELPER,
        $MERGE_HELPER_TEXT,
        $UTF8_NO_BOM
    )

    $PHASE = "canonical_merge"

    $env:PYTHONPATH = $PROJECT_ROOT

    & python `
        $MERGE_HELPER `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        $FEATURE_ID `
        $FEATURE_TITLE `
        $PATCH_ZIP `
        $EVIDENCE_FILE

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw (
            "Canonical Q01 freeze-evidence merge returned exit code " +
            $LASTEXITCODE
        )
    }

    $PHASE = "verify_latest_hint"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $LATEST_HINT -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "latest_freeze_hint.json was not created: $LATEST_HINT"
    }

    $LATEST_HINT_OBJECT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $LATEST_HINT `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8 |
        ConvertFrom-Json

    $LATEST_FEATURE_ID = [string](
        $LATEST_HINT_OBJECT.hint.feature_id
    )

    $LATEST_PATCH_ZIP = [string](
        $LATEST_HINT_OBJECT.hint.source_patch_zip
    )

    $LATEST_EVIDENCE = [string](
        $LATEST_HINT_OBJECT.form_inputs.validation_evidence_summary
    )

    if ($LATEST_FEATURE_ID -ne $FEATURE_ID) {
        throw (
            "Latest hint belongs to another feature: " +
            $LATEST_FEATURE_ID
        )
    }

    if ($LATEST_PATCH_ZIP -ne ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")) {
        throw (
            "Latest hint selected the wrong patch: " +
            $LATEST_PATCH_ZIP
        )
    }

    foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED_EVIDENCE_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $LATEST_EVIDENCE.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest hint evidence marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    $BLOCKED_MARKERS = @(
        "FREEZE BLOCKED",
        "Pre-validation delivery note only",
        "No recognizable validation evidence",
        "User-local validation is pending"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $BLOCKED_MARKERS) {
        if ($LATEST_EVIDENCE.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest hint contains blocked text: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MERGE_HELPER -PathType Leaf) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $MERGE_HELPER -Force
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q01 CANONICAL FREEZE EVIDENCE MERGE: PASS"
    Write-Host "FREEZE_HINT_SCAN: PASS"
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE_HINT_SELECTED_PATCH: " +
        $LATEST_PATCH_ZIP
    )
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE_HINT_EVIDENCE_MERGE_OK: " +
        $FEATURE_ID
    )
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE HINT MERGE OK: " +
        $FEATURE_ID
    )
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "LATEST HINT:"
    Write-Host $LATEST_HINT
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q01 CANONICAL FREEZE EVIDENCE MERGE FAILED"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q01 freeze remains blocked."
    Write-Host "Do not use Confirm and Write."
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the output, then press Enter"
Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
Clear-Host

1. Place the ZIP

Open a fresh PowerShell window. If the terminal displays >>, press Ctrl+C before pasting.
2. Install Q02

Paste this entire block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q02_visible_error_memory_preflight_enforcement_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q02-visible-error-memory-preflight-enforcement-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    $PHASE = "stage_zip"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Destination $STAGED_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $STAGED_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_ZIP or $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STAGED_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "install"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host ""

    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q02_visible_error_memory_preflight_enforcement_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q02-visible-error-memory-preflight-enforcement-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    foreach ($PATH in @(
        $STAGED_ZIP,
        $VALIDATOR,
        $FREEZE
    )) {
        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATH -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Required file was not found: $PATH"
        }
    }

    $PHASE = "validation"

    & $VALIDATOR `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

from pathlib import Path

        $PATCH_ZIP,
        $EVIDENCE_FILE,
        $MERGE_MODULE
    )) {
        if (-not (
            Test-Path `
                -LiteralPath $REQUIRED_PATH `
                -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Required file was not found: $REQUIRED_PATH"
        }
    }

    $PHASE = "validation_evidence_preflight"

    $EVIDENCE_TEXT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $EVIDENCE_FILE `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    $EVIDENCE_MARKERS = @(
        "ZIP CONTRACT: PASS",
        "Q02_BRICK_WALL_PREFLIGHT: PASS",
        "Q02_ROUTER_BRIDGE_ENFORCEMENT: PASS",
        "Q02_IMPLEMENTATION_GATE_LINK: PASS",
        "Q02_GENERATED_EXPORT_PREFLIGHT: PASS",
        "Q02_COMPACT_ALWAYS_READ_CONTRACT: PASS",
        "Q02_FULL_ZIP_OPEN_POLICY: PASS",
        "Q02_INCOMPLETE_RECORD_REJECTION: PASS",
        "Q02_METADATA_ALIGNMENT: PASS",
        "Q02_NO_NEW_ENGINE_OR_SCHEMA: PASS",
        "Q01_VERSION_ALIGNMENT: PASS",
        "Q01_METADATA_STAGE: PASS",
        "Q02_DURABLE_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE: PASS",
        "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID",
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $EVIDENCE_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $EVIDENCE_TEXT.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Durable validation marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    Write-Host "Q02 DURABLE VALIDATION EVIDENCE: PASS"

    $PHASE = "error_memory_staging_preflight"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path `
            -LiteralPath $LESSON_PATH `
            -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Staged Error Memory lesson was not found: $LESSON_PATH"
    }

    $LESSON_TEXT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $LESSON_PATH `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    if (-not $LESSON_TEXT.Contains(
        "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_BEGIN"
    )) {
        throw "Error Memory begin marker is missing."
    }

    if (-not $LESSON_TEXT.Contains(
        "KANDA_ERROR_LESSON_JSON_END"
    )) {
        throw "Error Memory end marker is missing."
    }

    if (-not $LESSON_TEXT.Contains(
        "lesson-brick-wall-q01-validator-forward-version-rigidity-v1"
    )) {
        throw "The staged lesson has the wrong lesson identity."
    }

    Write-Host "Q02_ERROR_MEMORY_INTAKE_STAGED: PASS"
    Write-Host "Q02_ERROR_MEMORY_LESSON_COUNT: 1"

    $PHASE = "create_merge_helper"

    $MERGE_HELPER_TEXT = @'
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import sys

project_root = Path(sys.argv[1]).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
project_root_text = str(project_root)

if project_root_text not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root_text)

from scripts.merge_freeze_validation_evidence import main

arguments = [
    "--project-root",
    project_root_text,
    "--feature-id",
    sys.argv[2],
    "--feature-title",
    sys.argv[3],
    "--patch-zip",
    sys.argv[4],
    "--evidence-file",
    sys.argv[5],
]

raise SystemExit(main(arguments))
'@

    $UTF8_NO_BOM = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(
        $MERGE_HELPER,
        $MERGE_HELPER_TEXT,
        $UTF8_NO_BOM
    )

    $PHASE = "canonical_freeze_evidence_merge"

    $env:PYTHONPATH = $PROJECT_ROOT

    & python `
        $MERGE_HELPER `
        $PROJECT_ROOT `
        $FEATURE_ID `
        $FEATURE_TITLE `
        $PATCH_ZIP `
        $EVIDENCE_FILE

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw (
            "Canonical Q02 evidence merge returned exit code " +
            $LASTEXITCODE
        )
    }

    $PHASE = "verify_latest_hint"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path `
            -LiteralPath $LATEST_HINT `
            -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "latest_freeze_hint.json was not created: $LATEST_HINT"
    }

    $HINT = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $LATEST_HINT `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8 |
        ConvertFrom-Json

    $HINT_FEATURE_ID = [string]$HINT.hint.feature_id
    $HINT_PATCH = [string]$HINT.hint.source_patch_zip
    $HINT_EVIDENCE = [string](
        $HINT.form_inputs.validation_evidence_summary
    )

    if ($HINT_FEATURE_ID -ne $FEATURE_ID) {
        throw (
            "Latest hint belongs to another feature: " +
            $HINT_FEATURE_ID
        )
    }

    if ($HINT_PATCH -ne ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")) {
        throw (
            "Latest hint selected the wrong patch: " +
            $HINT_PATCH
        )
    }

    foreach ($MARKER in $EVIDENCE_MARKERS) {
        if (-not $HINT_EVIDENCE.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest hint evidence marker missing: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    $BLOCKED_MARKERS = @(
        "FREEZE BLOCKED",
        "Pre-validation delivery note only",
        "No recognizable validation evidence",
        "User-local validation is pending"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $BLOCKED_MARKERS) {
        if ($HINT_EVIDENCE.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Latest hint contains blocked text: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MERGE_HELPER -PathType Leaf) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $MERGE_HELPER `
            -Force
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q02 CANONICAL FREEZE EVIDENCE MERGE: PASS"
    Write-Host "FREEZE_HINT_SCAN: PASS"
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE_HINT_SELECTED_PATCH: " +
        $HINT_PATCH
    )
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE_HINT_EVIDENCE_MERGE_OK: " +
        $FEATURE_ID
    )
    Write-Host (
        "FREEZE HINT MERGE OK: " +
        $FEATURE_ID
    )
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "LATEST HINT:"
    Write-Host $LATEST_HINT
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q02 FREEZE RECOVERY FAILED"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q02 freeze remains blocked."
    Write-Host "Do not use Confirm and Write."
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the output, then press Enter"
Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
Clear-Host

Q03 VERIFIED PROBLEM RECORD

Task classification:
Governed reliability repair

Change type:
REPAIR_EXISTING

Concrete problem:
Q03 requires every relevant Error Memory lesson to have an explicit,
executable regression disposition. Current KANDA source requires this in prose
but does not enforce a complete per-lesson regression-obligation matrix.

Evidence:
1. Brick Wall defines Q03 as an Error Memory regression-obligation matrix.
2. Brick Wall currently exposes only one free-text field:
   Regression obligations:
3. The governed implementation bridge says every relevant lesson must map to
   an existing validator, new focused regression test, justified
   non-applicability, or current-source reinterpretation.
4. The Error Memory exporter emits only the free-text
   Regression obligations: field.
5. validate_brick_wall_q02_error_memory_preflight_v1.py validates that the
   field exists, but it does not validate:
   - one matrix entry per relevant lesson;
   - duplicate lesson rows;
   - missing lesson dispositions;
   - valid disposition values;
   - validator or test paths;
   - non-applicability reasons;
   - expected success or rejection markers.

Practical impact:
A Q02 record may pass validation while one or more relevant lessons remain
advisory prose with no executable regression protection.

Existing KANDA capability inspected:
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/exporter.py
- brick_wall_comprehensive_quality_gate.md
- router_bridge_governed_implementation.md
- validate_brick_wall_q02_error_memory_preflight_v1.py

Why current capability is inadequate:
It validates the existence of the Regression obligations field, not the
completeness and disposition of every relevant lesson.

Duplication risk:
HIGH if a new Error Memory engine, schema, state owner, or regression subsystem
is created.

Smallest adequate intervention:
Strengthen the existing exporter and prompt owners with a structured textual
matrix contract, then add one focused validator. Do not create a new runtime
engine, persistent schema, GUI, or state owner.

Expected measurable gain:
A governed implementation cannot pass Q03 while any relevant Error Memory
lesson is missing an explicit regression disposition.

Disconfirming evidence searched:
Current exporter, Brick Wall prompt, governed implementation bridge, Q02
validator, metadata, and existing Error Memory workflow were inspected. No
existing validator was found that enforces a complete per-lesson matrix.

Admission decision:
REPAIR_EXISTING

May proceed:
YES, after Q02 closure actions

Planned bounded Q03 contract

Each relevant lesson will require one matrix entry containing:

Lesson ID:
Match confidence:
Freshness:
Disposition:
Protection owner:
Validator or test path:
Expected success marker:
Expected rejection marker:
Non-applicability or reinterpretation reason:
Status:

Allowed dispositions:

EXISTING_VALIDATOR
NEW_FOCUSED_TEST
NOT_APPLICABLE
CURRENT_SOURCE_REINTERPRETATION

The focused Q03 validator must reject:

a relevant lesson with no matrix row
duplicate lesson rows
an unknown disposition
EXISTING_VALIDATOR without a validator path
NEW_FOCUSED_TEST without a test path
NOT_APPLICABLE without a reason
CURRENT_SOURCE_REINTERPRETATION without current-source evidence
missing expected protection markers

1. Place the ZIP
