2. Install Q03

Paste this entire block at once:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q03_error_memory_regression_obligation_matrix_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q03-error-memory-regression-obligation-matrix-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"
    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    $PHASE = "stage_zip"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Destination $STAGED_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $STAGED_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_ZIP or $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STAGED_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "install"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK. Terminal will clear in 2 seconds..."

    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host ""

    Read-Host "Press Enter to clear terminal"
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    foreach ($PATH in @(
        $STAGED_ZIP,
        $VALIDATOR,
        $FREEZE
    )) {
        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATH -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Required file was not found: $PATH"

    & $VALIDATOR `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `

    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

}

Q04 VERIFIED PROBLEM RECORD

Task classification:
Governed reliability repair

Change type:
REPAIR_EXISTING

Concrete problem:
The current Q03 contract accepts a Freshness field containing one of
current / stale / partial / unresolved, but it does not enforce evidence that
the lesson was compared with current source.

Exact-source evidence:
1. exporter.py emits only:
   Freshness: current / stale / partial / unresolved
2. validate_brick_wall_q03_regression_obligation_matrix_v1.py checks that the
   Freshness field exists exactly once.
3. The Q03 validator does not verify:
   - referenced file existence;
   - referenced symbol existence;
   - current public facade;
   - current box and owner;
   - source fingerprint comparison;
   - lesson status;
   - superseded_by;
   - invalidation conditions.
4. guard.py filters lesson status and performs advisory text/fingerprint
   matching, but it does not inspect the current architecture or source owner.
5. Compact lessons already expose status, superseded_by, exception paths,
   fingerprints, triggers, and regression checks, but those values are not
   reconciled against current source before the lesson is classified fresh.

Practical impact:
A lesson may be marked current even when its file, symbol, facade, box,
ownership, or regression target has moved or disappeared.

Existing capability inspected:
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/exporter.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/guard.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/fingerprint.py
- kanda_reasoner_app/error_memory/models.py
- tools/validate_brick_wall_q03_regression_obligation_matrix_v1.py
- brick_wall_comprehensive_quality_gate.md
- router_bridge_governed_implementation.md

Why current capability is inadequate:
The current system captures a freshness conclusion but not the evidence needed
to derive or audit that conclusion.

Duplication or parallel-owner risk:
HIGH if Q04 creates a second Error Memory engine, lesson schema owner,
fingerprint engine, source indexer, or architecture scanner.

Smallest adequate intervention:
Strengthen the existing visible Error Memory preflight with one structured
freshness-verification block per relevant lesson and add one focused Q04
validator. Reuse existing compact lesson fields and exact-source inspection.
Do not create a new runtime engine or persistent schema.

Expected measurable gain:
Q04 cannot pass when a lesson is classified fresh without explicit,
source-grounded evidence for its current file, symbol, facade, owner, status,
supersession, fingerprint, and invalidation conditions.

Disconfirming evidence searched:
Existing guard matching, fingerprint generation, compact projection, exporter,
Q03 matrix validation, and prompt contracts were inspected. No existing
contract enforces complete source-grounded lesson freshness verification.

Admission decision:
REPAIR_EXISTING

May proceed to Error Memory preflight:
YES

1. lesson-brick-wall-q03-validator-package-import-context-v1

Best option: MEMORIZE

Reason: this lesson records a real implementation failure:

ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'kanda_reasoner_app'

The failure occurred because the validator dynamically loaded exporter.py without first establishing the project package import context.

This is reusable prevention guidance for future validators, isolated fixtures, installers, and dynamic-import tests. It should remain in Error Memory because the same mistake can recur in other boxes.

Expected prevention rule:

Before dynamically importing a project module, place the canonical project
root on sys.path and load the module through its package identity whenever
package-relative imports may exist.

Disposition:

MEMORIZE

2. lesson-brick-wall-q02-validator-forward-contract-rigidity-v1

Best option: MEMORIZE

Reason: this also records a real regression-design error. An older validator treated the exact Q02 contract text or version as permanently fixed, even though Q03 legitimately extended the contract while preserving all frozen Q02 behavior.

This is broadly useful because future Q04–Q40 releases will continue evolving the Brick Wall prompt and validators. Without this lesson, older validators may repeatedly fail whenever a compatible later contract is introduced.

Expected prevention rule:

Frozen validators must protect minimum behavioral contracts rather than require
permanent exact equality with an older prompt version or prose representation.
Later compatible versions are allowed only when every frozen behavioral marker
continues to pass.

Disposition:

MEMORIZE

Final recommendation

lesson-brick-wall-q03-validator-package-import-context-v1:
MEMORIZE

lesson-brick-wall-q02-validator-forward-contract-rigidity-v1:
MEMORIZE

1. INSTALL — POWERSHELL TERMINAL

Open a fresh PowerShell window and paste the entire block:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q04_error_memory_lesson_freshness_verification_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    $PHASE = "stage_zip"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Destination $STAGED_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $STAGED_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_ZIP or $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STAGED_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $INSTALLER -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "INSTALL.ps1 was not found: $INSTALLER"
    }

    $PHASE = "install"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED"
    Write-Host "INSTALL PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the complete output, then press Enter"

Do not continue unless this appears:

INSTALL OK: brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1

2. VALIDATE AND PREPARE FREEZE — POWERSHELL TERMINAL

Open another fresh PowerShell window:

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q04_error_memory_lesson_freshness_verification_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    foreach ($PATH in @(
        $STAGED_ZIP,
        $VALIDATOR,
        $FREEZE
    )) {
        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $PATH -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Required file was not found: $PATH"
        }
    }

    $PHASE = "validation"

    & $VALIDATOR `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Q04 validation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

    & $FREEZE `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Q04 freeze preparation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 VALIDATE + FREEZE PREPARATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 VALIDATION / FREEZE PREPARATION ERROR"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)
    Write-Host "Freeze remains blocked."
    Write-Host "Do not use Confirm and Write."
}

Write-Host ""
Read-Host "Copy the complete output, then press Enter"

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "kanda_brick_wall_q04_error_memory_lesson_freshness_verification_v1"
$FEATURE_ID = "brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"
$PHASE = "initialize"

try {
    $PHASE = "resolve_paths"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = (
        Resolve-Path `
            -LiteralPath $PROJECT_ROOT `
            -ErrorAction Stop
    ).Path

    $PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
    $DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)

    $WORK_DIR = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

    $ROOT_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $DRIVE_ROOT `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $STAGED_ZIP = Join-Path `
        $WORK_DIR `
        ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

    $EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $WORK_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    $PHASE = "stage_zip"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP -PathType Leaf) {
        Copy-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Destination $STAGED_ZIP `
            -Force

        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Patch ZIP staging failed: $STAGED_ZIP"
        }

        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $ROOT_ZIP `
            -Force

        Write-Host "PATCH ZIP STAGED: PASS"
        Write-Host "ROOT ZIP REMOVED: PASS"
    }

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP -PathType Leaf
    )) {
        throw "Patch ZIP was not found: $ROOT_ZIP or $STAGED_ZIP"
    }

    $PHASE = "zip_member_safety"

    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem

    $ARCHIVE = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead(
        $STAGED_ZIP
    )

    $SEEN = New-Object `
        "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" `
        ([System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)

    try {
        foreach ($ENTRY in $ARCHIVE.Entries) {
            $NAME = [string]$ENTRY.FullName

            if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($NAME)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: empty member name"
            }

            if ($NAME.IndexOf([char]0) -ge 0) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: null character"
            }

            $NORMALIZED = $NAME.Replace("\", "/")

            if (
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("/") -or
                $NORMALIZED.StartsWith("//") -or
                $NORMALIZED -match "^[A-Za-z]:"
            ) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: absolute member: $NAME"
            }

            if ($NORMALIZED.Split("/") -contains "..") {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_UNSAFE: traversal member: $NAME"
            }

            if (-not $SEEN.Add($NORMALIZED)) {
                throw "ZIP_ENTRY_DUPLICATE_OR_CASE_COLLISION: $NAME"
            }
        }
    }
    finally {
        $ARCHIVE.Dispose()
    }

    Write-Host "ZIP MEMBER SAFETY: PASS"

    $PHASE = "extract"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item `
            -LiteralPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
            -Recurse `
            -Force
    }

    New-Item `
        -ItemType Directory `
        -Path $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force | Out-Null

    Expand-Archive `
        -LiteralPath $STAGED_ZIP `
        -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR `
        -Force

    $INSTALLER = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "INSTALL.ps1"
    $VALIDATOR = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "VALIDATE.ps1"
    $FREEZE = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR "FREEZE.ps1"

    foreach ($REQUIRED_FILE in @(
        $INSTALLER,
        $VALIDATOR,
        $FREEZE
    )) {
        if (-not (
            Test-Path -LiteralPath $REQUIRED_FILE -PathType Leaf
        )) {
            throw "Required file was not found: $REQUIRED_FILE"
        }
    }

    $PHASE = "install"

    & $INSTALLER `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Q04 installation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: $FEATURE_ID"

    $PHASE = "validation"

    & $VALIDATOR `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -PatchZip $STAGED_ZIP `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Q04 validation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    $PHASE = "freeze_preparation"

    & $FREEZE `
        -ProjectRoot $PROJECT_ROOT `
        -NoTerminalCleanup

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Q04 freeze preparation returned exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 INSTALL: PASS"
    Write-Host "Q04 VALIDATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "Q04 FREEZE PREPARATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 LOCAL WORKFLOW FAILED"
    Write-Host "PHASE: $PHASE"
    Write-Host ("ERROR TYPE: " + $_.Exception.GetType().FullName)
    Write-Host ("ERROR MESSAGE: " + $_.Exception.Message)

}

& {
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

    $PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
    $WORKSPACE_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner\kanda_prompt_workspace"

    $FREEZE_ROOT = `
        "E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\project_freeze_after_update\frozen_features_memory\entries"

    $PATTERN = `
        "freeze-*-brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1-v*.md"

    $ENTRY = Get-ChildItem `
        -LiteralPath $FREEZE_ROOT `
        -Filter $PATTERN `
        -File |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTimeUtc -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    if (-not $ENTRY) {
        throw "Corrected Q04 freeze entry was not found."
    }

    $RAW = Get-Content `
        -LiteralPath $ENTRY.FullName `
        -Raw `
        -Encoding UTF8

    $REQUIRED = @(
        "Q04_FRESHNESS_CONTRACT: PASS"
        "Q04_GENERATED_EXPORT_FRESHNESS: PASS"
        "Q04_COMPLETE_FRESHNESS_ACCEPTED: PASS"
        "Q04_DURABLE_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE: PASS"
        "VALIDATION OK: brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    $FORBIDDEN = @(
        "Pre-validation delivery note only"
        "User-local validation is pending"
        "FREEZE BLOCKED"
    )

    foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED) {
        if (-not $RAW.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Corrected Q04 entry missing marker: $MARKER"
        }

        Write-Host ("Q04 FREEZE ENTRY MARKER: PASS - " + $MARKER)
    }

    foreach ($MARKER in $FORBIDDEN) {
        if ($RAW.Contains($MARKER)) {
            throw "Corrected Q04 entry contains forbidden text: $MARKER"
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host ("Q04 AUTHORITATIVE ENTRY: " + $ENTRY.FullName)
    Write-Host "Q04 AUTHORITATIVE FREEZE VERIFICATION: PASS"

    Set-Location -LiteralPath $WORKSPACE_ROOT

    & python `
        ".\prompt_tools\sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py" `
        "--ensure-sync" `
        "--yes" `
        "--project-root" `
        $PROJECT_ROOT

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup ensure-sync failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    & python `
        ".\prompt_tools\sync_startup_routing_kernel_pack.py" `
        "--check" `
        "--project-root" `
        $PROJECT_ROOT

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Startup check failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 POST-FREEZE STARTUP CONTEXT REFRESH: PASS"
    Write-Host "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
}

1. Search for the entry by content, not filename

Run this complete PowerShell block:

& {
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

    $FREEZE_ROOT = `
        "E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\project_freeze_after_update\frozen_features_memory"

    $FEATURE_ID = `
        "brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"

    $FEATURE_TITLE = `
        "Brick Wall Q04 Error Memory Lesson Freshness Verification v1"

    if (-not (
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $FREEZE_ROOT -PathType Container
    )) {
        throw "Frozen-memory root was not found: $FREEZE_ROOT"
    }

    $ALL_ENTRIES = Get-ChildItem `
        -LiteralPath $FREEZE_ROOT `
        -Recurse `
        -File `
        -Filter "*.md" `
        -ErrorAction Stop

    $Q04_ENTRIES = foreach ($FILE in $ALL_ENTRIES) {
        $RAW = Get-Content `
            -LiteralPath $FILE.FullName `
            -Raw `
            -Encoding UTF8 `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        if (
            $RAW.Contains($FEATURE_ID) -or
            $RAW.Contains($FEATURE_TITLE)
        ) {
            [pscustomobject]@{
                File = $FILE
                Raw = $RAW
            }
        }
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Q04 FREEZE ENTRY COUNT: $($Q04_ENTRIES.Count)"

    foreach ($ITEM in $Q04_ENTRIES) {
        Write-Host (
            "Q04 ENTRY FOUND: " +
            $ITEM.File.FullName
        )
    }

    $REQUIRED = @(
        "Q04_FRESHNESS_CONTRACT: PASS"
        "Q04_GENERATED_EXPORT_FRESHNESS: PASS"
        "Q04_COMPLETE_FRESHNESS_ACCEPTED: PASS"
        "Q04_DURABLE_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE: PASS"
        "VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID"
        "STATUS: IN_SYNC"
    )

    $FORBIDDEN = @(
        "Pre-validation delivery note only"
        "User-local validation is pending"
        "FREEZE BLOCKED"
    )

    $CLEAN_ENTRIES = @()

    foreach ($ITEM in $Q04_ENTRIES) {
        $MISSING = @()
        $BAD = @()

        foreach ($MARKER in $REQUIRED) {
            if (-not $ITEM.Raw.Contains($MARKER)) {
                $MISSING += $MARKER
            }
        }

        foreach ($MARKER in $FORBIDDEN) {
            if ($ITEM.Raw.Contains($MARKER)) {
                $BAD += $MARKER
            }
        }

        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host (
            "AUDIT ENTRY: " +
            $ITEM.File.FullName
        )

        if ($MISSING.Count -eq 0 -and $BAD.Count -eq 0) {
            Write-Host "Q04 ENTRY CLASSIFICATION: CLEAN"
            $CLEAN_ENTRIES += $ITEM
        }
        else {
            Write-Host "Q04 ENTRY CLASSIFICATION: NON-AUTHORITATIVE"

            foreach ($MARKER in $MISSING) {
                Write-Host (
                    "MISSING: " +
                    $MARKER
                )
            }

            foreach ($MARKER in $BAD) {
                Write-Host (
                    "FORBIDDEN: " +
                    $MARKER
                )
            }
        }
    }

    if ($CLEAN_ENTRIES.Count -eq 0) {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "Q04 CORRECTED FREEZE ENTRY: NOT FOUND"
        Write-Host "Q04 CONFIRM AND WRITE MUST BE REPEATED"
        Write-Host "Q05 REMAINS BLOCKED"
        return
    }

    $AUTHORITATIVE = $CLEAN_ENTRIES |
        Sort-Object {
            $_.File.LastWriteTimeUtc
        } -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host (
        "Q04 AUTHORITATIVE ENTRY: " +
        $AUTHORITATIVE.File.FullName
    )
    Write-Host "Q04 AUTHORITATIVE FREEZE VERIFICATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "MAY REFRESH STARTUP CONTEXT: YES"
}

2. Interpret the result

When the output says:

Q04 AUTHORITATIVE FREEZE VERIFICATION: PASS
MAY REFRESH STARTUP CONTEXT: YES

the entry exists and can be used even if its filename differs from the expected v2 name.

When it says:

Q04 CORRECTED FREEZE ENTRY: NOT FOUND
Q04 CONFIRM AND WRITE MUST BE REPEATED

the writer did not create the corrected entry.

In that case, open:

Then perform:

Run only after the interface confirms the write:

& {
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

    $FREEZE_ROOT = `
        "E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI\project_freeze_after_update\frozen_features_memory\entries"

    $FEATURE_ID = `
        "brick-wall-q04-error-memory-lesson-freshness-verification-v1"

    $FILES = Get-ChildItem `
        -LiteralPath $FREEZE_ROOT `
        -Filter "*.md" `
        -File `
        -ErrorAction Stop

    $CLEAN = @()

    foreach ($FILE in $FILES) {
        $RAW = Get-Content `
            -LiteralPath $FILE.FullName `
            -Raw `
            -Encoding UTF8

        if (-not $RAW.Contains($FEATURE_ID)) {
            continue
        }

        $HAS_REQUIRED = (
            $RAW.Contains("Q04_FRESHNESS_CONTRACT: PASS") -and
            $RAW.Contains("Q04_GENERATED_EXPORT_FRESHNESS: PASS") -and
            $RAW.Contains("Q04_COMPLETE_FRESHNESS_ACCEPTED: PASS") -and
            $RAW.Contains("Q04_DURABLE_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE: PASS") -and
            $RAW.Contains("VALIDATION OK: $FEATURE_ID") -and
            $RAW.Contains("STATUS: IN_SYNC")
        )

        $HAS_FORBIDDEN = (
            $RAW.Contains("Pre-validation delivery note only") -or
            $RAW.Contains("User-local validation is pending") -or
            $RAW.Contains("FREEZE BLOCKED")
        )

        if ($HAS_REQUIRED -and -not $HAS_FORBIDDEN) {
            $CLEAN += $FILE
        }
    }

    if ($CLEAN.Count -eq 0) {
        throw "No clean corrected Q04 freeze entry was found."
    }

    $AUTHORITATIVE = $CLEAN |
        Sort-Object LastWriteTimeUtc -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 1

    Write-Host "Q04 AUTHORITATIVE ENTRY: $($AUTHORITATIVE.FullName)"
    Write-Host "Q04 AUTHORITATIVE FREEZE VERIFICATION: PASS"
    Write-Host "MAY RUN POST-FREEZE STARTUP REFRESH: YES"
}

& {
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

    function Get-KandaRelativePath {
        param(
            [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
            [string]$BasePath,

            [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
            [string]$TargetPath
        )

        $SEPARATORS = [char[]]@(
            [System.IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar,
            [System.IO.Path]::AltDirectorySeparatorChar
        )

        $BASE_FULL = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
            $BasePath
        ).TrimEnd($SEPARATORS)

        $TARGET_FULL = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
            $TargetPath
        )

        if (
            $TARGET_FULL.Equals(
                $BASE_FULL,
                [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
            )
        ) {
            return "."
        }

        $PREFIX = (
            $BASE_FULL +
            [System.IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
        )

        if (
            -not $TARGET_FULL.StartsWith(
                $PREFIX,
                [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
            )
        ) {
            throw (
                "Target path escapes the expected base path. " +
                "Base: $BASE_FULL Target: $TARGET_FULL"
            )
        }

        return $TARGET_FULL.Substring($PREFIX.Length)
    }

    $SUPPORT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner_show_project_to_AI"

}
